def build_conv_matrix(f, n): # shape (2n-1,n), row k, col j = f[k-j]
    rowsize = 2*n-1
    data = np.empty(n*n, dtype=np.float64)
    cols = np.empty(n*n, dtype=np.int32) # max n maybe need int32 if n<2^31
    indptr = np.empty(rowsize+1, dtype=np.int64)
    pos=0
    indptr[0]=0
    ar = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)
    for k in range(rowsize):
        js = max(0, k-n+1)
        je = min(n-1, k)
        L = je-js+1
        # weights k-j descending from k-js to k-je
        # f_idx = np.arange(k-js, k-je-1, -1)
        data[pos:pos+L] = f[k-js : k-je-1 : -1]? Need if k-je could be negative? Let's test: n=5,k=2: js=0,je=2,k-js=2,k-je=0 -> f[2:0:-1] gives [f2,f1]? Missing f0 because stop exclusive. Python slice [start:stop:-1] stops before stop; need stop=-1? Simpler: idx = np.arange(k-js, k-je-1, -1) if k-je>=0 else np.arange(k-js, -1, -1). But k-je may be -? For k< n, js=0, je=k, k-je=0. We need indices k..0 inclusive. Use `np.arange(k-js, k-je-1, -1)` would for k=2: arange(2,-1,-1) -> [2,1,0] good if stop -1. k-je-1 = -1. For k=5,n5: js=1,je=4,k-js=4,k-je=1 -> indices [4,3,2]? But valid j1,2,3,4? Wait k=5, n=5, row5 (0-index), valid j from k-n+1=1 to min(4,5)=4 length4. We omitted j=0? js=1, k-j for j1..4 =4,3,2,1. Indices [4,3,2,1]. Formula k-je=1, stop k-je-1=0 -> arange(4,0,-1) = [4,3,2,1] good. General stop = k-je-1. If k-je can be -? For k < n, k-je=0, stop -1. Good.
        data[pos:pos+L]=f[idx]
        cols[pos:pos+L]=js+np.arange(L, dtype=np.int32)
        pos += L
        indptr[k+1]=pos
    A=csr_matrix((data, cols, indptr), shape=(rowsize,n))
```

Let's design subgradient more concretely.

`def subgrad_iteration(a, act_frac=0.0, eta_base=0.05, rng)`:
Input a positive vector sum S (not necessarily). Compute c = conv_fft(a). M=max(c). We can choose weights:
- If using active top fraction p (e.g., 0.01): threshold = np.percentile(c, 100*(1-p)). w = (c > threshold).astype(float); w = max(w - threshold + small,0)? Smooth.
- For non-smooth, w = (c >= M - 1e-6*M). Could be many. To smooth, use `w = np.exp((c - M)/tau)` normalized; this weights near max. tau ~0.1*(M-avg?) or M*0.1. Then compute grad = 2*corr(w,a). Normalize grad by max or sum.
- Update log amplitude: `loga = np.log(a + eps)`? Since a positive. `loga_new = loga - eta * grad_norm`; then `a_new = exp(loga_new) / exp(loga_new).mean() * sum(a)` to preserve sum? This can underflow if eta large. Instead multiplicative: `a_new = a * np.exp(-eta * (grad - grad.mean() + 0.5?))`. To exactly preserve sum, after update `a_new *= sum(a)/a_new.sum()`.
- Since objective scale invariant, preserving sum not critical but helps.

Gradient computation via FFT:
```
def corr(w,a): # h_j = sum_k w[k] a[k-j]
  L = next_fast_len( len(w)+len(a)-1)
  W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
  A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
  h = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), L)[:len(a)]
  return h
```

If subset LP, we can iteratively add constraints that became violated in the proposed g? This is like cutting plane. Procedure:
- Start active set A = indices where c_k close to M (top ~100-500).

- Check g: compute f*g; find violations for constraints not in A where (f*g)>M. Add the worst violators to A (or add top fraction). Repeat LP until no violations beyond tolerance or A size cap.

```
def active_lp_direction(f, M, n, max_rows=500, resolve_times=3):
    c = conv_fft(f)
    # initial rows = indices where c > M - 0.1*M or top k
    rows = np.where(c >= M - 0.05*M)[0]
    if len(rows) > max_rows: rows = top max_rows
    for it in range(resolve_times):
        g = solve_lp_rows(f, M, rows)
        if g failed: break
        # check f*g
        fg = conv_fft_linear? f*g with g, can use FFT (full convolution f,g)
        viol = fg - M
        if viol.max() <= tol: break
        bad = np.where(viol > tol)[0]
        # add top len(bad) or 100
        rows = np.unique(np.concatenate([rows, bad[top 100]]))
    return g
```

Let's design initial sequence factory:
```
def two_level_sequence(n, c=0.5, mass_ratio=2.0):
    L=int(round(c*n))
    a=np.zeros(n)
    m_left=mass_ratio/(1+mass_ratio)
    # solve A*L + B*(n-L) = S, A*L = m_left*S? Actually choose weights raw A,B: A/B = (m_left/L)/(m_right/(n-L))
    m_right=1-m_left
    A = m_left/L
    B = m_right/(n-L)
    a[:L]=A; a[L:]=B
    S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    a *= S/a.sum()
    return a
```
For c=0.5,mass_ratio=2 => A= (2/3)/L, B=(1/3)/(n-L). Good.

LP call:
```
def solve_lp_for_rows(f, M, rows, n, time_limit=30):
   A = build_row_matrix(f, rows, n)
   c_obj = -np.ones(n)
   b = np.full(len(rows), M)
   res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': time_limit, 'presolve': True})
   if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
      g=np.clip(res.x,0,None)
      # verify constraints maybe with FFT
      return g
   else return None
```
Since b M may be zero? no.
If presolve says unbounded due to not covered, ensure coverage catches.
If infeasible due to numerical, multiply b by 1.01 and retry.

- For t in [1.0, 0.8, 0.5, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02]: candidate = (1-t)*f + t*g_norm. Compute score_fast. Keep best candidate if < current* (1 - 1e-9). Also maybe if t=1 not valid, smaller.
- Return best candidate or None.

Update function:
```
def smooth_update(a, c, M, beta, eta, rng=None):
    # c maybe already computed
    x = c / max(beta,1e-12)
    # stable weights
    xmax = x.max()
    ex = np.exp(x - xmax)
    w = ex / ex.sum()
    grad = 2.0 * correlation(w, a)
    # normalize grad by sum or max to make eta scale independent
    gscale = np.max(np.abs(grad))
    if gscale==0: return a
    gradn = grad / gscale
    # center to preserve log-sum roughly? Use gradn - gradn.mean()
    # To avoid negative, exp could overflow; clip eta*range to 50
    adj = eta * (gradn - gradn.mean())
    adj = np.clip(adj, -50, 50)
    newa = a * np.exp(-adj)
    newa = np.clip(newa, 0, None)
    if newa.sum()<=0: newa=a
    S = a.sum()
    newa *= S/newa.sum()
    return newa
```

Let's outline final code:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
try: from scipy.optimize import linprog
except: linprog=None

def next_fast_len(m):
  # use scipy? implement power of 2
  p=1
  while p<m: p*=2
  return p

def conv_full(a):
  n=len(a)
  L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
  A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
  c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2*n-1]
  c[c<0 & c>-1e-12]=0? FFT errors small. return c

def score_fast(a):
  s=a.sum()
  if s<1e-12: return inf
  c=conv_full(a)
  M=c.max()
  return float(2*len(a)*M/(s*s))

def normalize(a, target=None):
  s=a.sum()
  if target is None: target=a.sum()
  if s<=0: return a
  return a*target/s
```

def active_lp_step(a, time_budget, max_rows=512, max_solves=3):
  S=a.sum()
  score,c,M=conv_and_score(a)
  rows = initial rows: top 128 + mid
  for solve_iter in range(max_solves):
    rows sorted unique, cap max_rows with mid included
    g = solve_lp_rows(a, M*(1+1e-7), rows, n, time_limit=20)
    if g is None: break
    fg=convolve_fft2(a,g)
    viol=fg - M*(1+1e-7)
    if viol.max() <= 1e-8*M: break
    bad = np.where(viol > 1e-8*M)[0]
    # add top bad by violation; if too many, take 128
    if len(bad)>128: order=bad[np.argsort(viol[bad])[-128:]]
    else order=bad
    rows = unique concat
    if len(rows)>max_rows: rows = choose top by max(c[rows], viol[rows]) plus mid
  if g is None: return None
  # line search on g
```

def try_update(base, g, S, current_score, ts=None):
  best=base; best_sc=current_score
  g_norm = g*S/g.sum()
  for gv in [g_norm, np.sort(g_norm)[::-1] if g_norm.shape]:
    for t in [1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
       cand=(1-t)*base + t*gv
       # floor, normalize if sum changed due to floor
       cand=regularize(cand, preserve_sum=S)
       sc=score_fast(cand)
       if sc < best_sc: best=cand; best_sc=sc
  return best,best_sc
```

Main `smooth_step`:
```
def smooth_step(a, beta, eta, S):
  c = conv_full(a); M=c.max()
  if M<=0: return a,M
  x = c / beta
  xmax=x.max(); ex=np.exp(x-xmax); w=ex/ex.sum()
  grad=2*correlation(w,a)
  gscale=grad.max()
  if gscale<=0: return a,M
  adj=eta*(grad/gscale - (grad/gscale).mean())
  adj=np.clip(adj,-10,10)
  cand=a*np.exp(-adj)
  return regularize(cand,S,sort=True?) 
```

Detailed code:
```
def fast_power_len(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p<<=1
    return p
```

Conv functions:
```
def convolution_fft(a, b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if b is None: b=a
    else: b=np.asarray(b,dtype=float64)
    n=len(a); m=len(b)
    L=fast_power_len(n+m-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b,L) if b is not a else A
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,L)
    return c[:n+m-1]
```
For square, use A*A. For different, `B=np.fft.rfft(b,L) if b is not a else A`. Need if a and b arrays different but equal object? Use flag.

`score_and_conv(a)`:
```
def score_and_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return inf, np.zeros(2*a.size-1), 0
    c=convolution_fft(a)
    M=float(c.max())
    score=float(2*a.size*M/(s*s))
    return score,c,M
```
If c has tiny negative, max positive. If M <=0 all zero, return inf? But sequence sum positive so some positive.

`regularize(a, S=None, sort_flag=False, floor_frac=1e-12)`:
```
arr=np.asarray(a,float64)
arr[~np.isfinite]=0
arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
if S is None: S=float(arr.sum())
if arr.sum()<=0:
   arr=np.ones_like(arr,dtype=float)*S/arr.size
   if S is None: S=arr.sum()
else:
   arr *= S/arr.sum()
if arr.size>0 and S>0:
   floor = max(1e-14*S/arr.size, np.finfo(float).tiny)
   # But if floor sum > S? floor tiny.
   arr = np.maximum(arr, floor)
   arr *= S/arr.sum()
if sort_flag:
   arr.sort()
   arr = arr[::-1]
   arr *= S/arr.sum()
return arr.copy()
```
If floor applied and many floor, sum may exceed S? But floor sum =1e-14 S negligible; renormalize reduces. Good.

   if alpha==0: uniform

def random_step(n,K,rng):
   br=np.sort(rng.random(K-1))*n
   br=np.concatenate([[0.0],br,[float(n)]])
   lengths=np.diff(br).astype(int)
   # ensure lengths nonzero: while zero? K small. If some zero, add 1? Simpler use np.repeat indices? 
   heights=np.sort(rng.lognormal(0,0.5,K))[::-1] + 0.1
   a=np.empty(n)
   pos=0
   for l,h in zip(lengths,heights):
      if l<=0: continue
      a[pos:pos+l]=h
      pos+=l
   if pos<n: a[pos:]=heights[-1]
   return a
```

Resampling height:
```
def resample_sequence(src, n):
   m=len(src)
   if m==n: arr=src
   else:
      x_old=np.arange(m,dtype=float)
      x_new=np.linspace(0,m-1,n)
      arr=np.interp(x_new,x_old,src.astype(float))
   return np.maximum(arr,0)
```

Now, smooth search implementation:
```
def smooth_search(a, score, deadline, rng, max_no_improve=5000):
    best=a; best_score=score
    S=best.sum()
    it=0; no_imp=0; last_report=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # choose beta/eta based on it
       c = convolution_fft(best)
       M = c.max()
       if M <=0: break
       # beta fractions cycle
       betas = [M/10.0, M/40.0, M/150.0, M/600.0]
       beta = betas[it % len(betas)]
       # weights
       x = c / beta
       xmax = x.max()
       ex = np.exp(x - xmax)
       wsum=ex.sum()
       if wsum<=0: w=np.ones_like(c)/c.size; else w=ex/wsum
       grad = 2.0 * correlation_fft(w,best)
       gmax = grad.max()
       if gmax <= 1e-18:
           # random
       else:
          gn = grad / gmax
          gm = gn.mean()
          # try multiple eta and sort
          improved=False
          for eta in [0.2,0.08,0.03,0.01,0.004]:
             adj = eta*(gn-gm)
             adj = np.clip(adj, -8, 8)
             raw = best * np.exp(-adj)
             # candidate unsorted and sorted
             for sf in (True, False):
                cand = regularize(raw, S, sort_flag=sf)
                sc = score_fast(cand)
                if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best=cand; best_score=sc; no_imp=0; improved=True; break
             if improved: break
          if not improved:
             no_imp += 1
       # if no improvement in this beta, maybe random perturb
       if no_imp > 50:
          raw = best + rng.normal(0, 0.01*S/n, n)
          # or block
          cand = regularize(raw,S,True)
          sc=score_fast(cand)
          if sc < best_score:
             best=cand; best_score=sc; no_imp=0
          else:
             # occasionally accept if small and temp
             temp = 1e-5 * (1 + (best_score-1.5))
             if sc < best_score + temp and rng.random() < np.exp(-(sc-best_score)/max(temp,1e-15)):
                best=cand; best_score=sc
             no_imp=0 # reset temp schedule?
       it +=1
       if it %200==0: print progress
    return best,best_score
```
This does up to 10 candidate evals per iteration; too many maybe. We can reduce: for each eta, only sorted candidate? Since we enforce monotone, use sorted always in smooth. But unsorted could occasionally better. We can try sorted first, if no improve try unsorted for first eta. Simpler: sorted only in smooth; local random includes sorting. The optimal likely monotone. Use sorted only to halve cost. Line search tried both in active LP. Good.

So smooth update sorted only. If sorting harms (unlikely), active LP can unsort.

- Compute gradient once per beta. Choose eta=0.1; candidate; if improves accept; else eta=0.03; else 0.01; stop. Max 3. Use sorted only.
Set etas [0.2,0.07,0.02,0.006]. 4.
Beta cycle maybe 1 per iter. Good.

Local search after smooth:
```
def local_search(a, score, deadline, rng):
  best=a; best_score=score; S=best.sum(); n=best.size
  temp=1e-5
  while time<deadline:
    # generate block perturb
    width = int(rng.integers(5, max(6,n//10)))
    st = int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
    raw=best.copy()
    raw[st:st+width] *= float(rng.lognormal(0,0.05))
    # maybe also global noise
    cand=regularize(raw,S,True)
    sc=score_fast(cand)
    if sc < best_score:
       best=cand; best_score=sc
    temp *=0.999
  return best,best_score
```
Can run many. Use block scaling; sorting after. This is like previous but monotone. It may polish.

Implement `active_lp_search(a, score, stop_time, rng)`:
```
def active_lp_search(a, score, stop_time, rng):
    if linprog is None: return a,score
    best=a; best_score=score; n=best.size; S=best.sum()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
       sc,c,M=score_and_conv(best)
       M_lp=M*1.0001
       rows0 = np.argsort(c)[-min(64,n//8):][::-1]
       # add rows above threshold
       thr = M - 0.2*(M - np.percentile(c,90)) maybe
       more = np.where(c > thr)[0]
       rows = unique concat rows0, more, [n-1]
       if len(rows)>LP_MAX_ROWS: rows= rows[:LP_MAX_ROWS]? But need sorted and top.
       # we need sorted unique for matrix. Use np.unique.
       g=None
       for sub in range(3):
          rows_sorted=np.unique(rows)
          # cap ensure mid
          if rows_sorted.size > max_rows:
             # choose top max_rows by c[rows_sorted], then add mid
             idx=np.argsort(c[rows_sorted])[-(max_rows-1):]
             sel=rows_sorted[idx]
             if (n-1) not in sel: sel=np.concatenate([sel,[n-1]])
             rows=sel
          A=build_sparse_rows(best, np.sort(rows), n)
          res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(len(rows),M_lp), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit':20})
          if res.x is not None:
             g=np.clip(res.x,0,None)
             if g.sum()>0:
                fg=convolution_fft(best,g)
                viol=fg-M_lp
                if viol.max() <= 1e-7*M_lp: break
                bad=np.where(viol>1e-7*M_lp)[0]
                if bad.size>128: bad=bad[np.argsort(viol[bad])[-128:]]
                rows=np.unique(np.concatenate([rows,bad]))
          else:
             g=None; break
       if g is None or g.sum()<=0: break
       gN = g*S/g.sum()
       # line search with sorted/unsorted
       improved=False
       for gv in (gN, np.sort(gN)[::-1]):
          gv = gv/gv.sum()*S
          gv = np.maximum(gv,0)
          for t in (1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02):
             raw=(1-t)*best + t*gv
             for sf in (True,False):
                cand=regularize(raw,S,sf)
                sc2=score_fast(cand)
                if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best=cand; best_score=sc2; improved=True; break
             if improved: break
          if improved: break
       it+=1
       if it %5==0: print
       if not improved and it>=10: break
    return best,best_score
```

```
def call_linprog(c_obj,A,b):
    try:
       res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,bounds=(0,None),method='highs',options={'time_limit':15,'presolve':True})
    except Exception:
       res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,bounds=(0,None),method='highs')
    return res
```

```
def convolution_fft(a, b=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if b is None:
       L=fast_power_len(2*a.size-1)
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)
       return c[:2*a.size-1]
    else:
       b=np.asarray(b,float)
       L=fast_power_len(a.size+b.size-1)
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); B=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,L)
       return c[:a.size+b.size-1]
```

`regularize` floor: If target_sum is None, we set S=arr.sum before floor. But then after floor, if S variable None? We'll implement:
```
def regularize(a, target_sum=None, sort_flag=False, floor_frac=1e-12):
    arr=np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    arr[~np.isfinite(arr)]=0
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
    total=float(arr.sum())
    if target_sum is None:
       target_sum=total
    if total <= 0 or not np.isfinite(total) or target_sum is None or target_sum <=0:
       arr.fill(1.0)
       target_sum = max(float(target_sum) if target_sum and np.isfinite(target_sum) else 1.0, 1e-12*arr.size)
    arr *= target_sum / total if total>0 else target_sum/arr.size
    if arr.size>0 and target_sum>0:
       floor = max(float(floor_frac)*target_sum/arr.size, np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
       # If floor * arr.size > target_sum, set floor=0
       if floor * arr.size > target_sum:
          floor = 0.0
       if floor>0:
          arr = np.maximum(arr, floor)
          s=arr.sum()
          if s>0: arr *= target_sum/s
    if sort_flag:
       arr.sort()
       if arr.size>0:
          # ensure descending
          arr = arr[::-1].copy()
          s=arr.sum()
          if s>0: arr *= target_sum/s
    return arr
```
If total=0, the line `arr *= target_sum / total` divide by zero. Handle separately:
```
if total<=0:
  arr.fill(target_sum/arr.size)
else: arr *= target_sum/total
```

`build_sparse_rows`: This can be expensive if called many times. Use row arrays. Let's code carefully:
```
def build_sparse_rows(f, rows, n):
    rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64)
    R=rows.size
    data_total = 0
    lengths = np.empty(R, dtype=np.int64)
    for i,k in enumerate(rows):
       js = int(max(0, k - n + 1))
       je = int(min(n - 1, k))
       L = je - js + 1
       lengths[i]=L
       data_total += L
    data=np.empty(data_total, dtype=np.float64)
    cols=np.empty(data_total, dtype=np.int32)
    indptr=np.empty(R+1, dtype=np.int64)
    pos=0; indptr[0]=0
    for i,k in enumerate(rows):
       L=int(lengths[i])
       if L>0:
          js=int(max(0, k-n+1)); je=int(min(n-1,k))
          start=k-js
          stop=k-je-1
          idx=np.arange(start, stop, -1, dtype=np.int64)
          data[pos:pos+L]=f[idx]
          cols[pos:pos+L]=js + np.arange(L, dtype=np.int32)
       pos += L
       indptr[i+1]=pos
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    return csr_matrix((data, cols, indptr), shape=(R,n))
```
If `stop` might equal start? L ensures stop < start. For L=1, start=stop+1, arange(start,stop,-1) -> [start]. Good.
We need import `csr_matrix` top-level. If scipy sparse not imported, import in try. We'll `from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix` in try with linprog. If fails, set None.

def filter_rows_by_score(rows, scores, max_rows, mid):
   rows=np.unique(rows)
   if rows.size <= max_rows:
      if mid not in rows: rows=np.concatenate([rows,[mid]])
   else:
      # score for mid maybe -inf, ensure
      order=np.argsort(scores[rows])
      keep=rows[order[-(max_rows-1):]]
      keep=np.unique(np.concatenate([keep,[mid]]))
   return np.sort(keep)
```

`smooth_search` code detail:
```
def smooth_search(a, score, stop_time, rng):
    best=np.array(a,dtype=float,copy=True)
    best_score=float(score)
    S=float(best.sum())
    n=best.size
    it=0
    no_imp=0
    last_print=t0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
       # compute c and M
       c=convolution_fft(best)
       # replace tiny negative? not needed
       M=float(c.max())
       if M<=0 or not np.isfinite(M): break
       # beta choice
       phase=it % 4
       if phase==0: beta=M/12.0
       elif phase==1: beta=M/50.0
       elif phase==2: beta=M/200.0
       else: beta=M/800.0
       if beta<=0: beta=M/50
       x=c/beta
       xmax=float(x.max())
       ex=np.exp(x-xmax)
       wsum=float(ex.sum())
       if wsum<=0 or not np.isfinite(wsum): w=np.ones_like(c)/c.size
       else: w=ex/wsum
       grad=2.0*correlation_fft(w,best)
       gmax=float(grad.max())
       improved=False
       if gmax>1e-15 and np.isfinite(gmax):
          gn=grad/gmax
          gm=float(gn.mean())
          etas=(0.25,0.1,0.04,0.015,0.006)
          for eta in etas:
             adj=eta*(gn-gm)
             adj=np.clip(adj, -10, 10)
             raw=best*np.exp(-adj)
             cand=regularize(raw,S,True)
             sc=score_fast(cand)
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best=cand; best_score=sc; no_imp=0; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          no_imp += 1
       # additional random moves if stuck
       if no_imp > 30:
          # block scale
          width=int(rng.integers(5, max(6, n//6)))
          st=int(rng.integers(0, n-width))
          raw=best.copy()
          raw[st:st+width] *= float(rng.lognormal(0.0, 0.05))
          cand=regularize(raw,S,True)
          sc=score_fast(cand)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
             best=cand; best_score=sc; no_imp=0
          else:
             # occasional anneal
             temp=1e-6 + 1e-4*min(1.0, (best_score-1.5))
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score + temp and float(rng.random()) < math.exp(-(sc-best_score)/max(temp,1e-15)):
                best=cand; best_score=sc
             no_imp=0
       it += 1
       if it % 500 == 0 and time.monotonic() - last_print > 10:
          print("smooth iter", it, "fast", best_score)
          last_print=time.monotonic()
    return best,best_score
```

def active_lp_search(a, score, stop_time, rng):
   if linprog is None or csr_matrix is None: return a,score
   best=...
   max_rows = max(128, min(1024, n//4))
   it=0; no_imp=0
   while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
      sc,c,M=score_and_conv(best)
      if not finite break
      M_lp=M*1.0002
      rows0=np.argsort(c)[-min(64,max(2,n//32)):][::-1]
      thr=M - 0.2*max(1e-12, M - float(np.percentile(c,90)))
      more=np.where(c > thr)[0]
      rows=np.unique(np.concatenate((rows0, more, np.array([n-1]))))
      g=None
      for sub in range(4):
         rows=filter_rows(rows,c,max_rows,n-1)
         try A=build_sparse_rows(best,rows,n)
         except: break
         b=np.full(rows.size,M_lp)

         except: break
         if res is not None and res.x is not None:
            g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
            g[~finite]=0; g=np.clip(g,0,1000)
            if g.sum()>0:
               fg=convolution_fft(best,g)
               viol=fg-M_lp
               if float(viol.max()) <= 1e-7*M_lp: break
               bad=np.where(viol > 1e-7*M_lp)[0]
               if bad.size>256: bad=bad[np.argsort(viol[bad])[-256:]]
               rows=np.unique(np.concatenate((rows,bad)))
         else: g=None; break
      if g is None or g.sum()<=0: break
      gN=g*S/g.sum(); gN=np.clip(gN,0,1000)
      improved=False
      g_sort=np.sort(gN)[::-1]
      for gv in (gN, g_sort):
         gv = gv/ gv.sum() * S if gv.sum()>0 else ...
         for t in (...):
             raw=(1-t)*best + t*gv
             for sf in (True,False):

                if sc2 < best_score:
                   best=cand; best_score=sc2; improved=True; break
             if improved: break
          if improved: break
      it+=1
      no_imp = 0 if improved else no_imp+1
      if it%5==0: print
      if no_imp > 8: break
   return best,best_score

`filter_rows`:
```
def filter_rows(rows, scores, max_rows, mid):
    rows=np.unique(np.asarray(rows,dtype=int))
    if rows.size ==0:
       return np.array([mid],dtype=int)
    if (max_rows <=0): max_rows=2
    if rows.size > max_rows:
       # keep top max_rows-1 by score
       k = max(1, max_rows-1)
       idx=np.argsort(scores[rows])[-k:]
       keep=rows[idx]
       if mid not in keep: keep=np.concatenate((keep,np.array([mid],dtype=int)))
    else:
       keep=rows
       if mid not in keep: keep=np.concatenate((keep,np.array([mid],dtype=int)))
    return np.sort(keep)
```
`mid not in keep` uses Python in on ndarray returns bool? `mid in keep` okay.

`call_linprog` top-level:
```
def call_linprog(c_obj,A,b):
   if linprog is None: return None
   try:
      return linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit':20,'presolve':True})
   except Exception:
      try:
         return linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
      except Exception:
         return None
```

def exact_score(arr):
    try:
       vals=[float(x) for x in arr]
       return float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
    except Exception:
       return float('inf')

Candidate function:
```
def make_product_power(n, alpha, beta):
    left=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    right=np.arange(n,0,-1,dtype=float)
    a=np.power(left, -alpha) * np.power(right, -beta)
    return a
```

`score_and_conv`: If a size large, `2 * len(a) * M / (s * s)`. If s=0, inf. If M negative? Use max(0).

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=max(0.0,float(budget_s))
    if total_budget < 90: return safe short? 
    reserve=60.0
    deadline=start + total_budget - reserve
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if total_budget < 300: N=1024
    elif total_budget < 900: N=2048
    else: N=4096
    S0=np.sqrt(2.0*N)
    a_init, fast_init = initial_candidates(N, min(start+40.0, deadline), rng)
    safe=make_safe_two_level(N,S0)
    # exact safe maybe if time
    best_exact_cand=None; best_exact_score=inf
    for cand in (a_init, safe):
       if time.monotonic() < start+10: sc=exact_score(cand); if finite update
    a_best=a_init; best_score=fast_init
    # Active LP
    if linprog is not None:
       lp_stop=min(deadline - 300.0, start + 300.0)
       if lp_stop > time.monotonic():
          a_lp, sc_lp = active_lp_search(a_best,best_score,lp_stop,rng)
          if sc_lp < best_score: update
    # Smooth
    smooth_stop=deadline
    a_smooth, sc_smooth = smooth_search(a_best,best_score,smooth_stop,rng)
    update
    # Final exact local
    S_best=a_best.sum()
    final_candidates=[a_best, safe, a_init]
    # add perturbations
    for _ in range(8): ...
    for cand in final_candidates:
       if time.monotonic() > start + total_budget - 3: break
       sc=exact_score(cand)
       if finite and sc < best_exact_score: update
    if best_exact_cand is None: best_exact_cand=a_best
    return [float(x) for x in best_exact_cand]

def mix_with_rational(a,n,alpha,mix, S):
   x=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
   raw=np.power(x, -alpha)
   raw=np.maximum(raw,0)
   S2=raw.sum()
   if S2>0: raw*=S/S2
   cand=(1-mix)*a + mix*raw
   return regularize(cand,S,True)

```python
"""
Monotone nonnegative step-function search for the minimum of
    2*n*max(a*a) / (sum(a))^2
under a*a = ordinary convolution.

Strategy:
- Initialize with strongly monotone analytic profiles, especially inverse-square-root
  tails and product-power profiles that empirically land near the 1.5 regime.
- Use fast FFT convolution for the objective gradient and line-search.
- Run an active-set LP direction finder (local subset of tight convolution constraints)
  as a higher-level move.
- Polish with smooth log-sum-exp subgradient descent and monotone block perturbations.
- Verify the final answer with the provided exact evaluator.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
except Exception:
    csr_matrix = None


def fast_power_len(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    p = 1
    m = int(max(1, m))
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def convolution_fft(a, b=None):
    """Linear convolution using FFT.  b defaults to a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if b is None:
        L = fast_power_len(2 * a.size - 1)
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
        return c[:2 * a.size - 1]

    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    L = fast_power_len(a.size + b.size - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, L)
    return c[:a.size + b.size - 1]


def correlation_fft(w, a):
    """
    h[j] = sum_k w[k] a[k-j], with a[k-j]=0 outside [0, len(a)-1].
    This is the gradient kernel for active-set convolution terms.
    """
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    L = fast_power_len(w.size + a.size - 1)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), L)
    return h[:a.size]


def score_and_conv(a):
    """Fast score and convolution values."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if n == 0 or s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(max(0, 2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    c = convolution_fft(a)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0
    score = float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))
    return score, c, M


def score_fast(a):
    return score_and_conv(a)[0]


def regularize(a, target_sum=None, sort_flag=False, floor_frac=1e-12):
    """
    Clip, optionally sort descending, and renormalize.
    Keeps a tiny positive floor to avoid numerical zeros in interior-point moves.
    """
    arr = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)

    total = float(arr.sum())
    if target_sum is None:
        target_sum = total

    if target_sum is None or not np.isfinite(target_sum) or target_sum <= 0.0:
        target_sum = 1.0

    if total <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(total):
        arr.fill(target_sum / max(1, arr.size))
    else:
        arr *= target_sum / total

    if arr.size > 0 and target_sum > 0.0:
        floor = max(float(floor_frac) * target_sum / float(arr.size),
                    np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
        if floor * arr.size > target_sum:
            floor = 0.0
        if floor > 0.0:
            arr = np.maximum(arr, floor)
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s > 0.0:
                arr *= target_sum / s

    if sort_flag:
        arr.sort()
        if arr.size > 0:
            arr = arr[::-1].copy()
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s > 0.0:
                arr *= target_sum / s
    return arr


def make_safe_two_level(n, target_sum):
    """
    A simple monotone two-level sequence with asymptotic score about 16/9.
    Used as a guaranteed fallback that improves on the all-ones score 2.
    """
    L = max(1, min(n - 1, int(round(0.5 * n))))
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:L] = 2.0
    return regularize(a, target_sum, sort_flag=True)


def make_raw_two_level(n, c, ratio):
    L = max(1, min(n - 1, int(round(c * n))))
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:L] = float(ratio)
    return a


def make_raw_power(n, alpha):
    idx = np.arange(n - 1, -1, -1, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.power(idx + 1.0, alpha) + 1e-6


def make_raw_rational(n, alpha):
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.power(idx, -float(alpha))


def make_raw_product_power(n, alpha, beta):
    left = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    right = np.arange(n, 0, -1, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.power(left, -float(alpha)) * np.power(right, -float(beta))


def make_raw_exp_gamma(n, beta, gamma):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n - 1)
    return np.exp(-float(beta) * np.power(x, float(gamma)))


def make_raw_sigmoid(n, beta, center):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n - 1)
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(float(beta) * (x - float(center))))


def make_raw_step_random(n, K, rng):
    br = np.sort(rng.random(K - 1)) * n
    br = np.concatenate(([0.0], br, [float(n)]))
    lengths = np.diff(br).astype(int)
    heights = np.sort(rng.lognormal(0.0, 0.55, size=K))[::-1] + 0.05
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    pos = 0
    for length, h in zip(lengths, heights):
        if length <= 0:
            continue
        a[pos:pos + length] = float(h)
        pos += length
    if pos < n:
        a[pos:] = float(heights[-1])
    return a


def resample_sequence(src, n):
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=np.float64)
    src = np.maximum(src, 0.0)
    src[~np.isfinite(src)] = 0.0
    m = src.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return src.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(src[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1, n)
    return np.interp(new, old, src)


def initial_candidates(n, stop_time, rng):
    """Build and evaluate a diverse set of monotone initial sequences."""
    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    S0 = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)

    raw_candidates = []

    # Two-level families.
    for c in (0.30, 0.35, 0.40, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.70):
        for r in (1.5, 2.0, 2.5, 3.0, 4.0, 6.0):
            raw_candidates.append(make_raw_two_level(n, c, r))

    # Power and rational profiles.
    for alpha in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        raw_candidates.append(make_raw_power(n, alpha))
    for alpha in (0.35, 0.42, 0.47, 0.49, 0.50, 0.51, 0.52, 0.55, 0.60):
        raw_candidates.append(make_raw_rational(n, alpha))
    for alpha in (0.40, 0.45, 0.48, 0.50, 0.52):
        for q in (2, 4, 16, 64, 256):
            idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
            raw_candidates.append(np.power(idx + float(q - 1), -float(alpha)))

    # Product powers.
    for alpha in (0.32, 0.38, 0.42, 0.45, 0.48, 0.50):
        for beta in (0.0, 0.02, 0.05, 0.08, 0.10):
            if alpha + beta <= 0.505:
                raw_candidates.append(make_raw_product_power(n, alpha, beta))

    # Smooth decays.
    for beta in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        for gamma in (0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5):
            raw_candidates.append(make_raw_exp_gamma(n, beta, gamma))
    for beta in (4.0, 10.0, 20.0, 40.0):
        for center in (0.45, 0.5, 0.55, 0.6):
            raw_candidates.append(make_raw_sigmoid(n, beta, center))

    # Existing global construction, if available.
    hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if hs is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.size > 0:
                arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
                arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
                if arr.size != n:
                    arr = resample_sequence(arr, n)
                raw_candidates.append(arr)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Evaluate fixed candidates.
    for raw in raw_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time:
            break
        cand = regularize(raw, S0, sort_flag=True)
        if cand.size != n or cand.sum() <= 0:
            continue
        sc = score_fast(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc

    # Random monotone step sequences.
    for _ in range(180):
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time:
            break
        K = int(rng.integers(4, 25))
        raw = make_raw_step_random(n, K, rng)
        cand = regularize(raw, S0, sort_flag=True)
        sc = score_fast(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc

    # Product-power finer grid, if time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < stop_time and best is not None:
        for alpha in np.linspace(0.32, 0.50, 19):
            max_beta = max(0.0, 0.50 - float(alpha))
            for beta in np.linspace(0.0, max_beta, 7):
                if time.monotonic() > stop_time:
                    break
                raw = make_raw_product_power(n, float(alpha), float(beta))
                cand = regularize(raw, S0, sort_flag=True)
                sc = score_fast(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best = cand
                    best_score = sc

    if best is None:
        best = make_safe_two_level(n, S0)
        best_score = score_fast(best)

    return best, float(best_score)


def build_sparse_rows(f, rows, n):
    """
    Build sparse matrix A with A[k_row, j] = f[k - j] for selected rows k.
    rows is sorted array of original convolution row indices 0..2n-2.
    """
    rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64)
    R = rows.size
    if R == 0:
        return csr_matrix((0, n))

    lengths = np.empty(R, dtype=np.int64)
    total = 0
    for i, k in enumerate(rows):
        js = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
        je = min(n - 1, int(k))
        L = je - js + 1
        lengths[i] = max(0, L)
        total += max(0, L)

    data = np.empty(total, dtype=np.float64)
    cols = np.empty(total, dtype=np.int32)
    indptr = np.empty(R + 1, dtype=np.int64)

    pos = 0
    indptr[0] = 0
    for i, k in enumerate(rows):
        L = int(lengths[i])
        if L > 0:
            js = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
            je = min(n - 1, int(k))
            start = int(k) - js
            stop = int(k) - je - 1
            idx = np.arange(start, stop, -1, dtype=np.int64)
            data[pos:pos + L] = f[idx]
            cols[pos:pos + L] = js + np.arange(L, dtype=np.int32)
        pos += L
        indptr[i + 1] = pos

    return csr_matrix((data, cols, indptr), shape=(R, n))


def call_linprog(c_obj, A, b):
    if linprog is None or csr_matrix is None:
        return None
    try:
        return linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=(0, None),
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": 25, "presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            return linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=(0, None),
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            return None


def filter_rows(rows, scores, max_rows, mid):
    """Keep at most max_rows most important rows, always keeping the middle row."""
    rows = np.unique(np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64))
    if rows.size == 0:
        return np.array([mid], dtype=np.int64)

    if max_rows < 2:
        max_rows = 2

    if mid not in rows:
        rows = np.concatenate((rows, np.array([mid], dtype=np.int64)))

    if rows.size > max_rows:
        keep_count = max(1, max_rows - 1)
        idx = np.argsort(scores[rows])[-keep_count:]
        keep = rows[idx]
        if mid not in keep:
            keep = np.concatenate((keep, np.array([mid], dtype=np.int64)))
        rows = keep

    return np.sort(np.unique(rows))


def active_lp_search(a, score, stop_time, rng):
    """
    Active-set LP direction search.

    For current f, solve approximate LPs:
        max sum(g)  s.t.  f*g <= M on selected tight rows, g >= 0.
    Then mix g into f and line-search.
    """
    if linprog is None or csr_matrix is None:
        return a, score

    best = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    best_score = float(score)
    S = float(best.sum())
    n = best.size
    max_rows = max(96, min(1024, n // 4))
    mid = n - 1

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
        sc, c, M = score_and_conv(best)
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        M_lp = M * 1.0002

        # Initial active rows: current near-max rows plus middle row.
        top_count = max(32, min(128, n // 32))
        rows0 = np.argsort(c)[-top_count:]
        more = np.where(c > 0.94 * M)[0]
        rows = np.unique(np.concatenate((rows0, more, np.array([mid], dtype=np.int64))))
        rows = filter_rows(rows, c, max_rows, mid)

        g = None
        for _sub in range(4):
            rows = filter_rows(rows, c, max_rows, mid)
            try:
                A = build_sparse_rows(best, rows, n)
            except Exception:
                A = None

            if A is None:
                break

            b = np.full(rows.size, M_lp, dtype=np.float64)
            res = call_linprog(-np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), A, b)

            if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
                g = None
                break

            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1000.0)

            if g.sum() <= 0.0:
                g = None
                break

            fg = convolution_fft(best, g)
            viol = fg - M_lp
            if float(viol.max()) <= 1e-7 * M_lp:
                break

            bad = np.where(viol > 1e-7 * M_lp)[0]
            if bad.size > 256:
                bad = bad[np.argsort(viol[bad])[-256:]]
            rows = np.unique(np.concatenate((rows, bad)))

        if g is None or g.sum() <= 0.0:
            break

        improved = False
        gN = g * (S / float(g.sum()))
        g_sort = np.sort(gN)[::-1]

        for gv in (gN, g_sort):
            gs = float(gv.sum())
            if gs <= 0.0:
                continue
            gv = gv * (S / gs)
            gv = np.maximum(gv, 0.0)

            for t in (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
                raw = (1.0 - t) * best + t * gv
                for sf in (True, False):
                    cand = regularize(raw, S, sort_flag=sf)
                    sc2 = score_fast(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best = cand
                        best_score = float(sc2)
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        it += 1
        no_improve = 0 if improved else no_improve + 1

        if it % 3 == 0:
            print(f"active_lp iter={it} fast={best_score:.9f}")

        if no_improve > 10:
            break

    return best, float(best_score)


def mix_with_rational(a, n, alpha, mix, S):
    """Convex mixture with a rational inverse-alpha profile."""
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    raw = np.power(idx, -float(alpha))
    raw = np.maximum(raw, 0.0)
    s = float(raw.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return regularize(a, S, sort_flag=True)
    raw *= S / s
    cand = (1.0 - float(mix)) * a + float(mix) * raw
    return regularize(cand, S, sort_flag=True)


def smooth_search(a, score, stop_time, rng):
    """
    Smooth log-sum-exp subgradient descent in log-space, with monotone projection.
    """
    best = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    best_score = float(score)
    S = float(best.sum())
    n = best.size

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
        c = convolution_fft(best)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        phase = it % 4
        if phase == 0:
            beta = M / 12.0
        elif phase == 1:
            beta = M / 50.0
        elif phase == 2:
            beta = M / 200.0
        else:
            beta = M / 800.0
        if beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
            beta = M / 50.0

        x = c / beta
        xmax = float(x.max())
        ex = np.exp(x - xmax)
        wsum = float(ex.sum())
        if wsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(wsum):
            w = np.ones_like(c, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
        else:
            w = ex / wsum

        grad = 2.0 * correlation_fft(w, best)
        gmax = float(grad.max())

        improved = False
        if gmax > 1e-15 and np.isfinite(gmax):
            gn = grad / gmax
            gm = float(gn.mean())

            # Backtracking-style eta schedule.
            for eta in (0.25, 0.10, 0.04, 0.015, 0.006):
                adj = eta * (gn - gm)
                adj = np.clip(adj, -10.0, 10.0)
                raw = best * np.exp(-adj)
                cand = regularize(raw, S, sort_flag=True)
                sc = score_fast(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = cand
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    no_improve = 0
                    improved = True
                    break

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1

        # Monotone local random moves.
        if no_improve > 25:
            width = int(rng.integers(5, max(6, n // 6)))
            st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width)))
            raw = best.copy()
            raw[st:st + width] *= float(rng.lognormal(0.0, 0.05))
            cand = regularize(raw, S, sort_flag=True)
            sc = score_fast(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = cand
                best_score = float(sc)
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                temp = 1e-6 + 1e-4 * max(0.0, min(1.0, best_score - 1.50))
                if (np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score + temp and
                        float(rng.random()) < math.exp(-(sc - best_score) / max(temp, 1e-15))):
                    best = cand
                    best_score = float(sc)
                no_improve = 0

        # Periodic escape from local basins.
        if no_improve > 1200:
            alpha = 0.48 + 0.05 * float(rng.random())
            for mix in (0.15, 0.35, 0.60):
                cand = mix_with_rational(best, n, alpha, mix, S)
                sc = score_fast(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best = cand
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    no_improve = 0
                    break
            no_improve = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 700 == 0 and (time.monotonic() - last_print > 15.0):
            print(f"smooth iter={it} fast={best_score:.9f}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best, float(best_score)


def exact_score(arr):
    """Authoritative score, using the externally provided evaluator."""
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in arr]
        return float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Returns a list of nonnegative heights.  Uses up to ~budget_s seconds (minus
    a small reserve for final exact evaluation).
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    # Very small fallback if something is wrong.
    if total_budget < 30.0:
        n = 256
        S0 = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
        return [float(x) for x in make_safe_two_level(n, S0)]

    # Reserve enough time for final exact scoring and perturbations.
    reserve = 70.0
    deadline = start + max(1.0, total_budget - reserve)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Choose length.  4096 is a good balance between asymptotic quality and
    # run-time / final exact convolution cost.
    if total_budget < 300.0:
        N = 1024
    elif total_budget < 900.0:
        N = 2048
    else:
        N = 4096

    S0 = math.sqrt(2.0 * N)

    # Initial shape search.
    init_stop = min(start + 45.0, deadline - 60.0)
    a_init, fast_init = initial_candidates(N, init_stop, rng)
    a_best = np.array(a_init, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    best_score = float(fast_init)
    safe = make_safe_two_level(N, S0)
    print(f"initial fast={best_score:.9f} n={N}")

    # Keep the best exactly-evaluated sequence.
    best_exact_cand = None
    best_exact_score = float("inf")

    for cand in (a_best, safe):
        if time.monotonic() < start + 12.0:
            sc = exact_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact_score:
                best_exact_score = float(sc)
                best_exact_cand = np.array(cand, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)

    # Active LP phase, if SciPy is available.
    if linprog is not None and csr_matrix is not None:
        lp_stop = min(deadline, start + 300.0)
        if lp_stop > time.monotonic():
            a_lp, sc_lp = active_lp_search(a_best, best_score, lp_stop, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc_lp) and sc_lp < best_score:
                a_best = a_lp
                best_score = float(sc_lp)
                print(f"after_active_lp fast={best_score:.9f}")

    # Smooth polish phase.
    a_sm, sc_sm = smooth_search(a_best, best_score, deadline, rng)
    if np.isfinite(sc_sm) and sc_sm < best_score:
        a_best = a_sm
        best_score = float(sc_sm)
    print(f"after_smooth fast={best_score:.9f}")

    # Final exact verification and small exact local search.
    S_best = float(a_best.sum())
    final_candidates = [a_best, safe, a_init]

    # A few small exact-verified perturbations around the best sequence.
    for _ in range(10):
        width = int(rng.integers(3, max(4, N // 20)))
        st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, N - width)))
        raw = a_best.copy()
        raw[st:st + width] *= float(rng.lognormal(0.0, 0.02))
        cand = regularize(raw, S_best, sort_flag=True)
        final_candidates.append(cand)

    for cand in final_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > start + total_budget - 3.0:
            break
        sc = exact_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = float(sc)
            best_exact_cand = np.array(cand, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
            print(f"final exact improved={best_exact_score:.9f}")

    if best_exact_cand is None:
        best_exact_cand = a_best

    # Final safety: guarantee finite, nonnegative, capped values.
    out = np.asarray(best_exact_cand, dtype=np.float64)
    out[~np.isfinite(out)] = 0.0
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if out.size == 0 or out.sum() <= 0.0:
        out = make_safe_two_level(256, math.sqrt(512.0))

    print("final fast estimate:", best_score)
    print("final exact score:", best_exact_score)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
